In this work, however, we explore a different approach by employing deep NNs to learn relationships among individual columns within a given table. We investigate whether NNs can predict the values of arbitrarily selected columns in a given table based on the remaining known columns. We call this problem In-Table Prediction (ITB), which is slightly different from table imputation methods and the pretraining task of TDL. Three potential usage scenarios are identified, which, to our best knowledge, have not been extensively studied in the literature. A self-supervised learning approach is applied to address this problem by randomly selecting columns to be masked out and used as learning targets. This work focuses on tabular datasets containing only continuous features. To handle missing values in continuous features, a novel neural layer is proposed to embed both numerical and empty values. Synthetic data is generated based on predefined column relationships, with empty values inserted using two distinct mechanisms. Additionally, an adapted masking strategy is employed to create test data. Performances of three NN architectures, namely MLP, Resnet and Transformer, are evaluated using the generated synthetic data. We conclude that, the attention-based structure outperforms the other two networks, when a sufficiently large number of training examples is available and a relatively large embedding length is chosen. We stress that these findings are obtained under controlled, synthetic conditions with a small number of columns and it should therefore be regarded as an initial, narrowly-scoped investigation rather than a general characterization of ITP on real-world tabular data.

</details>


### [203] [Position: Privacy Is a Claim, Not a Property of Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01273)
*Jiachen Zhao,Antonia Januszewicz,Taeho Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，指出合成数据在隐私敏感场景中的使用已从"在假定条件下的残余推理风险"悄然转变为"基于数据生成本身的表面属性"来认定隐私安全。作者通过分析主要ML会议的最新出版物，论证这种转变反映了社区对隐私证据标准的隐性变化，并建议将隐私视为明确的、基于证据的科学主张，要求隐私相关断言可界定、可测试、可质疑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据在机器学习研究中广泛使用，但其在隐私敏感场景中的隐私保证性质已悄然转变——从显式的推理风险声明变为由数据生成方式本身所暗示的表面属性，这导致隐私保证常常不清晰、难以验证且分布不均，引发了作者对社区标准变化的关注。

Method: 采用立场论文的方式，结合对近期主要ML会议出版物（如NeurIPS、ICML、ICLR等）的实证分析，考察合成数据在隐私敏感设置中的使用是否明确阐述威胁模型、推理风险或可证伪的隐私声明。

Result: 研究表明，合成数据在隐私敏感场景中被频繁使用，但往往缺乏对威胁模型、推理风险或可证伪隐私声明的明确阐述。隐私保证常是隐性的、难以验证且分布不均，尤其对稀有和少数群体记录暴露风险更高。

Conclusion: 作者主张应将隐私视为明确、基于证据的科学主张，而非隐含假设；建议ML会议采纳规范，要求隐私相关断言清晰界定范围、可测试且可被质疑，以提升隐私保证的严谨性和公平性。

Abstract: Synthetic data has become a common component of machine learning research. While widely adopted, its use in privacy-sensitive contexts has quietly shifted from a claim of residual inference risk under stated assumptions to an appearance-based property inferred from data generation itself. In this position paper, we argue that this shift reflects an implicit change in community standards for what counts as sufficient privacy evidence, rather than a misunderstanding of well-established privacy principles. Drawing on an empirical analysis of recent publications across major ML venues, we show that synthetic data is frequently used in privacy-sensitive settings without explicit articulation of threat models, inference risks, or falsifiable privacy claims. As a result, privacy assurance often remains implicit, difficult to verify, and unevenly distributed, with heightened exposure for rare and minority records. We argue for treating privacy as an explicit, evidence-based scientific claim and recommend that ML venues adopt norms requiring privacy-relevant assertions to be clearly scoped, testable, and contestable.

</details>


### [204] [The Constitutional Coverage Trilemma in AI Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01275)
*Natalija Mitic,Soona Sedahmed A. O.,Mamadou Selly Ly,Moustapha Cisse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过审计23个前沿大语言模型的默认宪法（隐含价值排序）并对1649名美国参与者进行成对权衡研究，发现用户对安全、帮助性、诚实、自主性和公平性五大价值的需求广泛分布，但AI系统的"宪法供给"狭窄且漂移——23个原型仅覆盖约2%的需求空间，无任何原型将帮助性或自主性置于首位，且自主性价值在多数模型家族中持续下降。研究提出一个2顶点菜单方案即可超越全部23个原型的表现，并将发现形式化为"预算化多元主义三难困境"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿AI系统本质上是"宪法性制度"，每个部署模型都编码了安全、帮助性、诚实、自主性和公平性之间的隐含排序。核心问题是：AI系统的"宪法类型"供给是否覆盖了人类对这些价值的需求？作者旨在量化供给与需求之间的匹配缺口及其影响。

Method: 采用双管齐下的方法：(1) 对23个前沿LLM原型的出厂默认宪法进行同义改写控制的审计；(2) 使用相同的工具对1649名美国参与者进行成对权衡研究。结合噪声匹配估计、距离加权福利分析和排序验证等方法对供给-需求空间进行形式化分析。

Result: 三个核心发现：(1) 需求广泛——五大价值均有支持者，最大群体占比不足三分之一；(2) 供给狭窄且漂移——23个原型只覆盖约2%的需求空间，没有原型把帮助性或自主性放在首位，37%的用户"无家可归"，且跨模型家族中自主性下降（5/6）、公平性上升（5/6）、安全性上升（4/6）；(3) 修正方案稀疏——2顶点菜单在平均遗憾上胜过全部23个原型47%，3个顶点加入可将均值和最差群体遗憾降低最多81%/64%。

Conclusion: AI模型的宪法供给与人类社会需求之间存在系统性错配，且错配方向具有方向性危害——漂移正远离已覆盖不足的价值（自主性），机制性地恶化了最少服务用户的福利下限。作者通过"预算化多元主义三难困境"的形式化框架表明该约束状态在经验上真实存在，并提出稀疏菜单是有效且可行的修正方案。

Abstract: Frontier AI systems function as \emph{constitutional institutions}: each deployed model encodes an implicit ranking among safety, helpfulness, honesty, autonomy, and equity. We ask whether the supply of frontier constitutional types covers human demand. Combining a paraphrase-controlled audit of the as-shipped default constitutions of $23$ frontier LLM archetypes with a pairwise-tradeoff study of $1{,}649$ US participants on the same instrument, we report three facts. \emph{Demand is broad}: it spans all five values, with the largest constituency under one-third. \emph{Supply is narrow and drifting}: the $23$-archetype hull occupies ${\sim}2\%$ of the demand hull under conservative noise-matched estimation ($0.10\%$ at full audit precision), no archetype puts helpfulness or autonomy first ($37\%$ of users are constitutionally homeless), and across six model families autonomy decreases in $5/6$, equity increases in $5/6$, and safety increases in $4/6$, with monotone within-family version trends (order-permutation $p = 0.013$) and the autonomy decline concentrated in scenarios where safety is not at stake. The drift's importance is directional: \emph{away} from a value already undercovered, mechanically worsening the welfare floor for the least-served users. \emph{The fix is sparse}: a $2$-vertex menu $\{e_{\mathrm{HON}}, e_{\mathrm{AUT}}\}$ beats the full $23$-archetype frontier by $47\%$ on mean regret (CI $[43\%, 52\%]$); three vertex additions cut mean/worst-group regret by up to $81\%$/$64\%$. We formalize these findings as a budgeted-pluralism trilemma, show the binding regime is empirically realized, and verify the conclusions are robust to distance-based welfare and to degraded routing. The instrument and audit harness are described in full in the appendices.

</details>


### [205] [One-Layer Transformer Provably Learns Multiclass One-Nearest Neighbor in Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01311)
*Skanda Athreya,Yutong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将单层Transformer与最近邻分类器在二分类场景下的等价性研究扩展到多分类场景，证明使用argmax分类头的单层Transformer在多分类设置下与一对一近邻分类器行为完全一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作已在二分类场景下建立了单层Transformer与最近邻分类器之间的等价性，但在多分类场景下的结果依赖于非标准的基于取整的方法，而非实践中常用的argmax分类头，留下了这一理论空白。本文旨在填补这一缺口。

Method: 利用单纯形编码（simplex encoding），将单层Transformer与多分类场景下的最近邻分类行为进行理论对齐。通过数学分析证明，配备argmax分类头的单层Transformer在多分类任务中与一对一近邻（1-NN）分类器具有完全相同的行为。

Result: 成功证明在argmax分类头下，单层Transformer与多分类一对一近邻分类器行为等价，弥合了先前工作中因采用非标准的取整方法而留下的理论差距。

Conclusion: 该研究完善了Transformer与最近邻分类器等价性的理论框架，将其从二分类完整推广到多分类场景，并使得理论结果与实际使用的模型架构（argmax头）保持一致。

Abstract: We extend recent work establishing an equivalence between one-layer transformers and nearest-neighbor classifiers in the binary setting to the multiclass case. By leveraging the simplex encoding, we show that one-layer transformers with an argmax classification head behave identically to a one-nearest-neighbor classifier in the multiclass setting. This closes a gap left by prior work, whose multiclass result relied on a non-standard rounding-based approach rather than the typical argmax head used in practice.

</details>


### [206] [Bandits in Prod: Hyperparameter Optimization at Inference Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01335)
*Louis Abraham,Tuan-Anh Nguyen,Nicolas Devatine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在线超参数优化（OHPO）框架IMABO，将生产环境中仅能通过线上请求和噪声反馈评估配置的问题形式化为无限臂老虎机问题，并引入IMOSS策略及多种配置提议器，在多样化的OHPO场景中取得最低累积遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多生产系统（尤其是现代智能体系统）只能在实时请求上评估配置并观察噪声反馈，且缺乏代表性验证数据，这给超参数优化带来了根本性挑战。现有方法难以处理这种在线、无验证集的环境，需要一个正式的框架来应对这一场景。

Method: 将OHPO形式化为混合与条件搜索空间上的无限多臂老虎机问题。提出通用框架IMABO，将任意（a）用于选择已采样配置的bandit策略与（b）用于提出新配置的oracle相结合。实例化IMOSS策略（无重启、任意时间、活动集以t^β增长），并组合三种实用oracle：树结构Parzen估计器、基于逐坐标bandit的现方案变异oracle、以及预训练表格基础模型。

Result: 证明IMOSS的期望累积分位数遗憾界为O(p_ρ^{-1/β} + T^{(1+β)/2})。三种oracle均优于均匀随机oracle基线。IMABO在从经典机器学习模型调参到LLM智能体配置的多样化OHPO设置中获得最低累积遗憾。

Conclusion: IMABO为缺乏验证数据的生产系统（特别是LLM智能体系统）提供了一种通用的在线配置优化方法论，通过bandit策略与提议oracle的解耦设计实现了理论保障与实践效果的统一。

Abstract: Many production systems can assess a configuration only by using it on live requests and observing noisy feedback. Modern agentic systems are a prominent example, with inference-time choices such as model selection, retrieval depth, prompting strategy, and decoding temperature, yet often with no representative validation data. We formalize this setting as Online Hyperparameter Optimization (OHPO) and cast it as an infinitely many-armed bandit over mixed and conditional search spaces. We introduce IMABO, a general framework that combines any bandit policy for choosing among already sampled configurations with any oracle for proposing new ones. We instantiate it with IMOSS, a restart-free anytime policy whose active set grows as $t^β$, and prove an expected cumulative quantile-regret bound of $O(p_ρ^{-1/β} + T^{(1+β)/2})$, where $β\in(0,1)$ controls active-set growth and $p_ρ$ lower-bounds the probability that a proposed configuration falls in the top-$ρ$ fraction of the search space. We combine IMOSS with three practical oracles: a Tree-structured Parzen Estimator, an incumbent-mutation oracle driven by a per-coordinate bandit, and a pretrained tabular foundation model, all three improving over the uniform random oracle baseline. IMABO obtains the lowest cumulative regret across diverse OHPO settings, from tuning classical machine-learning models to configuring LLM-based agents.

</details>


### [207] [SMELT: Scaling Laws for Compute-Matched MoE Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01343)
*Shaowen Wang,Ge Zhang,Kairong Luo,Yuhao Wu,Shaofan Liu,Jiaheng Liu,Wenhao Huang,Shen Yan,Jian Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SMELT（稀疏MoE Transformer中半层循环两次）方法，在匹配每次token的FLOPs、总非嵌入参数和KV缓存三个预算的前提下，将Transformer中间一半的层循环执行两次。作者将模型扩展到高达540亿非嵌入参数，并拟合了Chinchilla风格的缩放定律，发现SMELT在计算最优前沿上节省6.8%-18.0%的训练FLOPs，且优势在代码任务和长序列场景中更为显著。机制分析表明，第二次循环访问减少了注意力汇聚（attention sink）效应，将注意力权重重新分配到内容相关token上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前对循环Transformer的研究大多在固定模型大小下进行比较，混淆了架构优势与额外计算量（FLOPs）带来的收益。作者希望在严格匹配计算预算的前提下，公平评估循环架构是否真的能带来性能提升，并探索其在稀疏MoE架构上的应用潜力。

Method: 在匹配per-token FLOPs、总非嵌入参数和KV cache三个预算的条件下，通过一系列消融实验确定最优配方：将MoE Transformer中间一半的层循环两次（SMELT），与不循环的Baseline对齐所有预算。将SMELT扩展到四种规模（最大54B非嵌入参数），对两种架构分别拟合Chinchilla风格缩放定律，并在下游基准、不同样本长度和上下文示例数量下评估，同时进行机制层面的注意力分析。

Result: SMELT的损失随计算量的增加下降更快，在计算最优前沿上节省6.8%-18.0%的训练FLOPs。性能优势超出验证损失预测的下游基准表现，在代码任务上最大，并随样本长度和上下文示例数量增加而增强。机制分析发现第二次层访问减少了注意力汇聚现象，将注意力质量转向内容相关token。

Conclusion: 在严格匹配计算预算的条件下，层循环仍能为Transformer带来可衡量的性能提升，验证了深度复用作为实用的架构改进方向的价值，并为稀疏MoE模型的层循环提供了一种可直接采用的配方（SMELT）。

Abstract: Looped Transformers increase effective depth by iterating a shared block of layers, but most evaluations compare at fixed model size, conflating architectural advantage with extra FLOPs. We study looping on Mixture-of-Experts Transformers while closely matching per-token FLOPs, total non-embedding parameters, and KV cache. Through a series of ablations, we arrive at a recipe we call SMELT (Sparse MoE Transformer, middle layers Loop Twice), which loops the middle half of layers twice while matching the unlooped Baseline on all three budgets. We scale SMELT across four sizes up to 54B non-embedding parameters and fit a separate Chinchilla-style scaling law for each architecture. SMELT's loss drops faster with compute, saving 6.8--18.0\% of training FLOPs on the compute-optimal frontier. The advantage transfers to downstream benchmarks beyond what validation loss predicts, is largest on Code, and grows with sample length and the number of in-context examples. Mechanistic analysis shows that the second visit reduces the attention sink and redirects mass toward content-relevant tokens, an inductive bias that may underlie the observed performance gains. These results show that looping can improve Transformers even under budget matching, offering a practical recipe that turns depth reuse into measurable gains.

</details>


### [208] [Contribution-Aware Bandwidth Allocation for Multimodal Split Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01406)
*Iason Ofeidis,Leandros Tassiulas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ModalShare，一种面向多模态分割学习的上行带宽分配器。它通过Shapley贡献分数为每种模态分配不同的保持比率，在固定上行预算下优化多模态训练的准确性，相比均等分配在CREMA-D和MVSA上分别提升15.4和12.4个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模型越来越多地部署在边缘设备，但训练几乎全在数据中心完成，因为单台客户端无法承载每种模态的编码器。Split Learning使这种训练可行，但代价是上行链路每一轮都必须传输所有模态的破碎激活。现有压缩方案给每种模态相同的保持比率，导致共享预算按激活维度比例分配，而这种分配方式与每种模态对融合预测的实际贡献无关。

Method: ModalShare将模态间的带宽分配视为显式决策，称为跨模态分配。在固定上行预算下，每种策略传输相同的期望载荷，区别仅在于载荷如何在模态间拆分。分配器基于Shapley贡献分数为每种模态设置保持比率，该分数由服务器利用已接收的激活联盟计算得出，不增加上行流量和客户端计算，也无需预先知道流类型。

Result: 在5倍压缩下，ModalShare相比均等保持比率在CREMA-D上提升15.4个百分点，在MVSA上提升12.4个百分点。在三种压缩器、三个数据集和四个预算设置下均表现优异，并证明现有压缩器在多模态场景下表现不佳，ModalShare能够恢复被遗漏的增益。

Conclusion: 模态间分配是多模态Split Learning中一个被忽视但重要的优化维度。ModalShare通过利用服务器端已有的Shapley贡献信息进行智能带宽分配，在不增加任何额外开销的前提下显著提升多模态分布式训练的精度。

Abstract: Multimodal models are increasingly the default option for perception at the network edge, yet they are trained almost entirely in the datacenter, because a client holding several sensor streams cannot host an encoder per modality. Split Learning makes such training feasible by keeping only the first layers on the device, at the cost of an uplink that must carry smashed activations for every modality at every step. Existing compression schemes give each modality the same keep-ratio, so the shared budget is divided in proportion to smashed-activation dimension, a quantity unrelated to how much each modality contributes to the fused prediction. We make that division an explicit decision and call it inter-modality allocation: under a fixed uplink budget, every policy transmits the same expected payload and differs only in how that payload is split across modalities. Our allocator, ModalShare, sets each modality's keep-ratio from a Shapley contribution score that the server computes over coalitions of activations it has already received. Measuring this score adds no uplink traffic and no client-side computation, and needs no prior knowledge of which stream is which. ModalShare improves accuracy over equal keep-ratios by 15.4 and 12.4 percentage points on CREMA-D and MVSA at matched payload in 5x compression, with strong performance across three compressors, three datasets, and four budgets. We show that existing compressors underperform in multimodal settings, with ModalShare recovering what gains are left behind.

</details>


### [209] [Predicting Subsurface Abnormalities Growth using Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01417)
*Mehrdad Shafiei Dizaji,Hoda Azari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将物理信息神经网络（PINNs）引入探地雷达（GPR）数据预测领域，提出了一个结合CNN、空间特征通道注意力（SFCA）、ConvLSTM和时间特征帧注意力（TFFA）的深度学习模型，通过将电磁波传播物理规律嵌入网络架构，实现了对GPR数据的精确预测，可有效用于桥梁面板状况评估等土木基础设施检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的GPR数据预测方法在精度上存在局限，作者受医学影像预测模型启发，希望将物理定律与深度学习相结合，提升土木基础设施（如桥梁面板）无损评估（NDE）的预测精度，并深入理解劣化机制。

Method: 模型由三个组件构成：CNN提取空间特征、空间特征通道注意力（SFCA）机制自适应计算通道注意力权重以优化视觉特征响应，以及结合ConvLSTM与时间特征帧注意力（TFFA）模块来提取和精调时间序列特征，同时将电磁波传播的物理规律直接嵌入神经网络架构中。

Result: 实验结果表明，将物理知识直接整合到神经网络中显著提高了GPR数据的预测精度，这对于桥梁面板条件评估及其他土木基础设施评估具有重要价值。

Conclusion: PINNs在无损评估（NDE）领域展现出变革性潜力，不仅能提升基础设施劣化预测的精度，还能通过物理模型视角加深对劣化基本机制的理解。

Abstract: The research explores the pioneering integration of Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) into the domain of Ground-Penetrating Radar (GPR) data prediction. This research presents a detailed development framework for a specialized PINN model, proficient at interpreting and forecasting GPR data, much like how medical imaging models predict tumor behavior. By harnessing the synergy between deep learning algorithms and the physical laws governing subsurface structures or in medical terms, human tissues the model effectively embeds the physics of electromagnetic wave propagation into its architecture. This ensures that predictions not only align with fundamental physical principles but also mirror the precision needed in medical diagnostics for detecting and monitoring tumors. The suggested deep learning structure comprises three components: a CNN, a spatial feature channel attention (SFCA) mechanism, and ConvLSTM, along with temporal feature frame attention (TFFA) modules. The attention mechanism computes channel attention and temporal attention weights using self-adaptation, thereby fine tuning the visual and temporal feature responses to extract the most pertinent and significant visual and temporal features. By integrating physics directly into the neural network, our model has shown enhanced accuracy in forecasting GPR data. This improvement is vital for conducting effective assessments of bridge deck conditions and other evaluations related to civil infrastructure. The use of Physics Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) has demonstrated the potential to transform the field of Non-Destructive Evaluation (NDE) by enhancing the precision of infrastructure deterioration predictions. Moreover, it offers a deeper insight into the fundamental mechanisms of deterioration, viewed through the prism of physics-based models.

</details>


### [210] [Provably Safe Sim-to-Real Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01418)
*Tingting Ni,Maryam Kamgarpour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对安全模拟到现实迁移问题，在奖励无关安全强化学习框架下设计了一种计算高效算法，利用模拟器信息显著减少真实世界交互，同时保证安全探索并能针对任何潜在奖励函数计算近似最优可行策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界强化学习样本成本高昂，通常在模拟器中训练策略再迁移到现实，但模拟器与现实之间存在失配会导致策略次优。修正失配需要收集真实数据，然而在机器人、医疗等领域，数据收集本身受安全约束限制，因此需要解决安全约束下的模拟到现实迁移问题。

Method: 将安全模拟到现实迁移问题形式化为奖励无关安全强化学习框架内的任务，设计了一种计算高效的算法，能够利用模拟器信息来证明性地减少真实世界交互次数，同时保证探索过程的安全性，并支持针对任意潜在奖励函数求解近似最优可行策略。

Result: 给出了真实世界样本复杂度界，该界刻画了使用模拟器的收益与模拟到现实失配程度之间的关系，证明了算法在减少真实数据需求的同时保证了安全性和最优性。

Conclusion: 该工作为安全模拟到现实迁移提供了理论保障的算法框架，证明了利用不完美模拟器可以在保证安全的前提下显著降低真实世界样本复杂度，样本复杂度随模拟器与现实失配程度呈现相应变化。

Abstract: To mitigate the sample complexity of real-world reinforcement learning (RL), a common practice is to first train a policy in a simulator, where samples are cheap, and then deploy the learned policy in the real world with the hope that it generalizes effectively. Such direct sim-to-real transfer is not guaranteed to succeed: simulator-trained policies can be suboptimal in the real world due to sim-to-real mismatch. Correcting this mismatch requires collecting data from the real system, but in many applications, such as robotics and healthcare, this data-collection process is itself subject to safety constraints. This gives rise to the problem of safe sim-to-real transfer: how can an agent exploit an imperfect simulator while ensuring safe real-world data collection and learning a near-optimal feasible policy for the target system? We address this problem by formulating safe sim-to-real transfer within the framework of reward-free safe RL. We design a computationally efficient algorithm that exploits simulator information to provably reduce real-world interaction while ensuring safe exploration and enabling the computation of a near-optimal feasible policy for any potential reward function. Our real-world sample complexity bound characterizes the benefit of using the simulator in terms of the sim-to-real mismatch.

</details>


### [211] [CATeye: Coupled Attribute-Topology Invariance Learning for Voucher Abuse Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01425)
*Tian Tian,Shuaicheng Niu,Hao Kuang,Yuanhang Hu,Dong Li,Zhiqi Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CATeye框架，通过耦合的属性-拓扑不变性学习解决电商优惠券滥用检测中因欺诈模式演变导致的分布偏移问题，在两个数据集上显著优于九个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商平台中优惠券滥用欺诈模式随时间与地域快速演变，导致分布偏移使现有检测模型性能退化且需频繁重训练。关键在于属性偏移通过属性相似性构建的边耦合传导至拓扑结构，经GNN消息传递进一步放大变体信号，使问题更具挑战性。

Method: CATeye包含两个可学习选择器：(1) 属性不变性选择器(AIS)学习节点自适应掩码以过滤非不变属性；(2) 边不变性选择器(EIS)基于保留的不变属性采样不变子图并隔离非不变边。利用不变与非不变组件构建多个视图，通过视图特定目标增强域不变表示并抑制域特定变化。

Result: 在Lazada专有数据集和公开基准上的实验表明，CATeye一致优于九个强领域泛化与图异常检测基线，平均F1分数最高比最强基线提升8.61%。

Conclusion: CATeye通过解耦并选择性地保留属性与拓扑层面的不变信息，有效应对耦合的属性-拓扑分布偏移，为电商优惠券滥用检测提供了可靠的领域泛化解决方案。

Abstract: Voucher abuse poses a major challenge in e-commerce, where malicious users exploit promotional vouchers for profit. Unfortunately, fraud patterns evolve rapidly over time and across regions, causing distribution shifts that degrade existing detection models unless retrained frequently. To tackle this, we propose the Coupled Attribute-Topology Invariance Learning framework (CATeye). The key challenge arises from coupled attribute-topology shift, where edges built from attribute proximity cause environment-driven attribute shift to induce shifted topology, thereby amplifying variant signals through GNN message passing. CATeye sees through such coupled shifts with two learnable selectors. First, an Attribute Invariance Selector (AIS) learns node-adaptive masks to filter out non-invariant attributes. Then, conditioned on retained invariant attributes, an Edge Invariance Selector (EIS) samples an invariant subgraph and isolates non-invariant edges. Using the resulting invariant and non-invariant components, CATeye constructs multiple views and applies view-specific objectives to emphasize domain-invariant representations while suppressing domain-specific variations. Experiments on both a proprietary dataset from Lazada, a major Southeast Asian e-commerce platform, and a public benchmark show that CATeye consistently outperforms nine strong domain generalization and graph anomaly detection baselines, achieving up to an 8.61% improvement in average F1 score over the strongest baseline. Source code is publicly available at https://github.com/Tian0426/CATeye.

</details>


### [212] [TRIAGE: Three-level Routing and Intelligent Agent Guidance for Efficient Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01428)
*Ruocan Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TRIAGE，一个基于三层路由的LLM代理框架，通过TaaS（Trajectory-as-a-Skill）机制将历史执行轨迹抽象为可复用技能，实现"经验即服务"。在1007条安全监控查询中节省62.3% token，跨域ToolBench验证节省76.3% token，并展现出"越用越高效"的正反馈学习特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ReAct范式的LLM代理存在根本性效率问题：每次查询都从头执行完整推理循环，相似查询会重复相同步骤，无法利用历史经验，导致大量token浪费。

Method: 提出TRIAGE三层路由框架：(1) Direct Reuse——完全相同的查询直接复用，消耗0 token；(2) Skill Substitution——相似查询通过确定性参数替换执行，消耗0 token；(3) Full ReAct——全新查询正常执行并自动存入历史。核心创新是TaaS将历史轨迹提炼为可复用技能，并配备自动Skill提取机制，从高频轨迹模式中蒸馏确定性技能形成正反馈循环。

Result: 在1007条安全监控查询上实现62.3% token节省，其中56.0%查询落在Level 2、5.5%落在Level 1，均为零成本执行。在ToolBench的15个领域345条查询上跨域验证达到76.3% token削减。在线学习实验显示前100条查询内L2命中率从0%升至57%，平均token成本从198降至74.7。

Conclusion: TRIAGE通过历史轨迹复用有效解决了ReAct代理的效率瓶颈，语义路由具有良好的跨域泛化能力，且具备冷启动到成熟的进化能力，实现了"越用越高效"的自增强效率提升机制。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents based on the ReAct paradigm have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in tool use and task execution. However, ReAct suffers from a fundamental efficiency problem: every query triggers a complete reasoning loop from scratch, and similar queries repeat identical steps without leveraging historical experience. We propose TRIAGE,a three-level routing framework that reduces token consumption by reusing historical execution trajectories. Its core innovation is TaaS (Trajectory-as-a-Skill), which abstracts historical execution trajectories into reusable skills, realizing 'experience as a service'. TRIAGE classifies queries into three levels: (1) Direct Reuse-identical queries, 0 tokens; (2) Skill Substitution-similar queries, 0 tokens via deterministic parameter substitution; (3) Full ReAct-novel queries, automatically stored for future reuse. In large-scale experiments on 1,007 security monitoring queries, TRIAGE achieves 62.3% token savings, with 56.0% of queries at Level 2 and 5.5% at Level 1, both executing at zero cost. Cross-domain validation on ToolBench (15 domains, 345 queries) achieves 76.3% token reduction, confirming the generalizability of semantic routing. An online learning experiment demonstrates cold-start-to-mature evolution: the L2 hit rate rises from 0% to 57% within the first 100 queries, and the average token cost drops from 198 to 74.7. We also propose an automatic Skill extraction mechanism that distills high-frequency trajectory patterns into deterministic Skills, creating a positive feedback loop of 'the more you use it, the more efficient it becomes'.

</details>


### [213] [Learning Sparse Decision Trees via Transformer Variational Auto-Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01430)
*Giacomo Fidone,Alessio Cascione,Riccardo Guidotti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TREVIS是一种基于树Transformer变分自编码器（TTVAE）学习决策树的方法，通过将决策树映射到潜在连续空间，利用梯度优化来联合优化预测性能和结构稀疏性等复杂目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大多数决策树学习算法仅关注预测性能，忽略了结构稀疏性等其他理想属性的联合优化。虽然决策树因其透明性在高风险决策场景中广受欢迎，但缺乏能够同时优化多个复杂目标的学习方法。

Method: TREVIS利用树Transformer变分自编码器（TTVAE）将决策树映射为潜在表示，将离散的搜索空间转化为连续空间，通过可微分的代理模型实现基于梯度的优化，从而学习满足复杂目标的决策树。

Result: 实验表明，TREVIS学习出的决策树在预测性能上与现有的近最优算法相当，同时显著改善了树的结构稀疏性。

Conclusion: TREVIS为决策树学习提供了一种新的范式，能够在连续潜在空间中实现多目标联合优化，在保持预测性能的同时获得更稀疏、更结构化的决策树，适用于对可解释性要求高的场景。

Abstract: Decision trees are among the most widely used models in machine learning, largely due to their transparent decision logic, making them well-suited for high-stakes decision-making contexts. However, most existing learning algorithms focus on predictive performance, overlooking the joint optimization of other desirable properties, such as structural sparsity. In this work we propose TREVIS, an approach for learning decision trees with respect to complex objectives, based on the exploration of the latent space of a Tree Transformer Variational Auto-Encoder (TTVAE). By mapping decision trees onto latent representations, TREVIS replaces the discrete search space with a continuous one, enabling gradient-based optimization via a differentiable surrogate model. We experiment with TREVIS for learning decision trees that jointly optimize predictive performance and sparsity. Results show that TREVIS discovers decision trees matching the predictive performance of existing near-optimal algorithms while improving their structural sparsity.

</details>


### [214] [Efficiently Estimating Optimal Hyperparameter Scaling Laws through Power-Law Entropy Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01431)
*Zhiliang Chen,Sebastian Ament,David Eriksson,Maximilian Balandat,Eytan Bakshy,Jihao Andreas Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为Power-Law Entropy Search (PLES)的采集函数，基于多保真贝叶斯优化，通过自适应实验高效估计大语言模型训练中最佳超参数的最优缩放规律，相比传统网格搜索可节省超过90%的计算预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上估计最优超参数缩放规律需要对数千次训练运行进行穷举网格搜索，消耗大量计算资源，难以在真实生产规模下高效应用。

Method: 提出PLES，一种计算成本感知的采集函数，建立在多保真贝叶斯优化框架上。其核心创新在于搜索能降低缩放规律估计整体不确定性的候选配置，而非优化单一目标函数；每轮迭代选择在单位计算成本下最大程度减少缩放规律不确定性的配置，自然倾向于选择信息量高的小规模实验。

Result: 在合成基准、基于真实LLM训练数据的代理模型以及实际LLM预训练实验中验证，PLES在所有设置下均能以不到传统网格搜索和其他基线十分之一的计算预算收敛到准确的最优超参数缩放规律。

Conclusion: PLES证明了成本感知的贝叶斯优化方法能够高效、准确地估计最优超参数缩放规律，显著降低大规模LLM训练配置调优的计算开销，为该领域提供了高效可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Optimal hyperparameter scaling laws describe how the best hyperparameters for large language model (LLM) training change with model and data scale, enabling practitioners to predict optimal configurations at production scales without expensive large-scale tuning. However, estimating these scaling laws conventionally requires exhaustive grid searches over thousands of training runs, consuming enormous computational resources. We introduce Power-Law Entropy Search (PLES), a computational cost-aware acquisition function built on multi-fidelity Bayesian optimization that efficiently estimates optimal hyperparameter scaling laws through adaptive experimentation. A key innovation in PLES is that it searches for candidates that reduce the overall uncertainty of a scaling law estimate, instead of optimizing a single objective function. At each iteration, PLES selects the candidate configuration that maximally reduces the uncertainty of the scaling law estimates per unit computational cost, naturally favoring informative small-scale experiments. We evaluate PLES on synthetic benchmarks, surrogate models fitted to real LLM training data, and actual LLM pre-training runs. Across all settings, PLES converges to accurate optimal hyperparameter scaling laws using less than one-tenth of the computational budget required by conventional grid search and other baselines.

</details>


### [215] [Edge-Girth as a Structural Edge Feature for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01441)
*Lilian Marey,Charlotte Laclau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于边围长（edge-girth）及其重数作为逐边结构描述符，注入门控消息传递架构（EGAGNN），无需预先指定子结构大小即可捕获任意长度的最短环，在ZINC-12k基准上MAE显著低于门控基线；同时证明在图鉴别任务上，当所有边具有相同的最短环长度和重数时，该描述符退化为常数，模型回落到1-WL表达能力上限，BREC基准中90个此类图对均无法区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于消息传递的GNN无法超越1-WL测试的表达能力，常用的补救方法是向节点或边特征注入预计算的子结构计数（如三角形或长环计数），但这些方法需要预先人为设定所计数的子结构大小，而这种设定通常缺乏数据依据，需要一种无需预先承诺子结构大小的结构描述符。

Method: 定义边的边围长为经过该边的最短环长度，其重数为该最短环的数量，两者构成逐边不变特征，可通过每条边的一次广度优先搜索精确计算。将该描述符注入门控消息传递架构（EGAGNN）中；同时理论分析该描述符的表达能力边界，研究其恒定性条件及其与1-WL的关系。

Result: 在ZINC-12k回归基准上，EGAGNN以104k参数达到的测试MAE比最接近的门控比较器低三倍；与同架构下受限环计数描述符相比，仅在字典需计数到长度8的环时才被匹配（且使用两倍通道数），而字典只计到长度为4时效果与无结构信息相当。在图鉴别方面，当图中每条边看到相同数量相同长度的最短环时，描述符恒定，任何基于该描述符的模型回落到1-WL界限；BREC基准中400对图里90个此类图对全部无法区分。

Conclusion: 边围长及其重数是一种无需预先指定子结构大小即可探测任意长度最短环的有效描述符，在回归任务上优于固定上限的结构计数方法；但其表达能力存在匹配的理论限制——对于各边最短环信息一致的图类，该描述符退化为常数，无法超越1-WL。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNN) based on message passing are provably no more powerful than the one-dimensional Weisfeiler--Leman colour-refinement test (1-WL): two graphs it cannot tell apart receive identical representations, however deep or wide the network. A common remedy augments node or edge features with precomputed structural descriptors, most often counts of a fixed small subgraph such as triangles or longer cycles, but such counts require committing in advance to the size of the substructure counted, a choice usually made blind to the data. We study a descriptor that avoids this choice. The edge-girth of an edge is the length of a shortest cycle through it, and its multiplicity is the number of such shortest cycles; together they form a per-edge invariant that reports cycles of arbitrary length, computable exactly by a single breadth-first search per edge. Injected into a gated message-passing architecture, EGAGNN, it reaches a test MAE a factor three below the closest gated comparator on the ZINC-12k regression benchmark at 104k parameters; against bounded cycle-counting descriptors under the same architecture, it matches only a dictionary counting cycles up to length eight, using twice as many channels, while a dictionary capped at length four performs no better than no structural information at all. On graph discrimination we prove a matching limitation: on graphs where every edge sees the same number of shortest cycles of the same length, the descriptor becomes constant and any model built on it collapses back to the 1-WL bound. This holds without exception across all 400 pairs of the BREC benchmark: not one of the 90 such pairs is distinguished.

</details>


### [216] [Diffusion as a Training Curriculum for Timestep-Free Iterative Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01449)
*Mariia Drozdova,Aidan Sirbu,Pietro Miotti,Robert Obryk,Mayalen Etcheverry,Eyvind Niklasson,Blake Richards*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将扩散模型与递归推理器结合，在扩散去噪器中加入持久隐状态并移除时间步条件，得到一个可任意深度运行的统一更新。该任意时刻求解器在Sudoku-Extreme上达到99.90%的精确求解率，在Maze-Unique上达到98.93%。关键发现是推理时无需渐进去噪——每步注入最大噪声仍能保持近完美求解，而训练时的有序退火腐蚀至关重要，表明扩散的核心贡献在于去噪训练课程而非推理采样过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型和递归推理器都是迭代过程，但二者跨迭代传递信息的方式不同。作者希望将两者的优势结合：扩散模型的逐步去噪训练策略与递归推理器的深度推理能力，构建一个无需外部验证器或并行采样即可高效探索解空间的任意时刻求解器。

Method: 1) 在扩散去噪器中加入持久隐藏状态，移除时间步条件化，使网络变为单一共享更新，可运行至任意深度；2) 训练时保留有序退火腐蚀（扩散训练课程）；3) 推理时采用固定最大噪声注入策略——每个非线索变量每步替换为新鲜高斯噪声，无需渐进退火。

Result: 在Sudoku-Extreme上达到99.90%的精确求解率，在Maze-Unique上达到98.93%的求解率，且精度随推理深度持续提升，远超训练时的展开长度和反向传播窗口。最大噪声注入策略在推理时保持近完美求解并能收敛到稳定解。

Conclusion: 扩散模型对该任意时刻求解器的主要贡献不在于推理时的采样过程，而在于其去噪训练课程。简单的噪声注入机制即可使单条轨迹高效探索解空间并收敛至正确答案，无需先验推理模型所需的并行展开、候选选择或外部验证器。

Abstract: Diffusion models and recursive reasoners are both iterative, but they carry information across iterations differently. We add a persistent hidden state to a diffusion denoiser and remove its timestep conditioning, leaving a single shared update that can be run to arbitrary depth. The result is an anytime solver: accuracy keeps improving with inference depth far beyond the rollout lengths and backpropagation window used in training, reaching 99.90% exact solve on Sudoku-Extreme. We also obtain 98.93% solve rate on Maze-Unique. Surprisingly, progressive denoising is unnecessary at inference: holding corruption at its maximum by replacing every non-clue variable with fresh Gaussian noise at each step retains near-perfect solving and converges to stable solutions. This simple noise-injection mechanism enables a single trajectory to efficiently explore the solution space and settle on the correct answer without parallel rollouts, candidate selection, or external verifiers required by prior reasoning models. Nonetheless, ordered annealed corruption remains critical during training, which suggests that diffusion's primary contribution to our anytime solver is not a sampling procedure at inference, but a denoising training curriculum.

</details>


### [217] [Rethinking Learnability in Offline Data-driven Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01493)
*Chao Qian,Chen-Guang Wang,Rong-Xi Tan,Ke Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出算法依赖可学习性（algorithm-dependent learnability）概念，证明其值查询形式在离散优化（如子模最大化的贪心与局部搜索）和连续凸优化中的充足性，并据此构建一个轨迹学习框架，提出UGTL方法（不确定性感知的梯度引导轨迹学习）。UGTL在Design-Bench五个任务上以3.1/25的聚合平均排名超越其他24种方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线数据驱动优化（仅利用固定历史评估数据寻找高质量解）虽有大量方法被提出，但一个根本性理论问题悬而未决：离线优化需要怎样的可学习性？此前研究表明PAC可学习性不足——即使大部分区域被学习得很好，最优区域仍可能被学习得很差。需要一个更贴合优化过程本质的可学习性刻画。

Method: 首先提出算法依赖可学习性，要求仅在优化器的搜索轨迹上具有准确性；证明其值查询形式对子模最大化的贪心与局部搜索（离散场景）以及投影梯度下降（凸最小化）是充分的。由此形式化轨迹学习框架（轨迹构建、轨迹建模、候选生成），并在此框架下分析现有方法。进一步提出UGTL：通过不确定性感知的梯度引导构造局部一致的改进轨迹，用条件扩散模型建模，并选取多样化候选集。

Result: 在Design-Bench的五个任务上，UGTL在25种方法中取得最佳聚合平均排名3.1/25。通过受控轨迹分析和跨架构替换实验，验证了所提轨迹构建设计是性能提升的关键因素。

Conclusion: 工作从理论上回答了离线优化所需的学习条件，并从实践上以轨迹学习框架给出统一视角，明确提出与有效性验证了UGTL方法。轨迹构造对离线优化的性能提升具有关键作用。

Abstract: Black-Box Optimization (BBO) has found broad applications, but evolutionary algorithms and Bayesian optimization face efficiency challenges as real-world BBO problems grow increasingly complex. Data-driven optimization improves the efficiency of BBO algorithms by learning from data. Offline data-driven optimization seeks high-quality solutions using only a fixed set of previous evaluations, attracting substantial attention because it requires no additional online evaluations. Many offline optimization methods have been proposed, but a fundamental question remains unanswered: what learnability is sufficient for offline optimization? Prior theoretical studies show that Probably Approximately Correct (PAC) learnability is insufficient, as the optimal region may remain poorly learned even when most regions are well learned. In this paper, we propose algorithm-dependent learnability, which requires accuracy only on the optimizer's trajectory. We prove that its value-query form is sufficient for representative discrete settings, including greedy and local search for submodular maximization, while its first-order analogue is sufficient for projected gradient descent on convex minimization. Motivated by this notion, we formalize a trajectory-learning framework comprising trajectory construction, trajectory modeling, and candidate generation, and analyze existing trajectory-based methods under it. We further propose Uncertainty-aware Gradient-guided Trajectory Learning (UGTL), which constructs locally coherent improvement trajectories reflecting plausible search paths, models them with conditional diffusion, and selects a diverse candidate set. On five Design-Bench tasks, UGTL achieves the best aggregate mean rank, $3.1/25$, among 25 methods. Controlled trajectory analyses and cross-architecture replacements confirm that our trajectory construction plays a significant role in the improvement.

</details>


### [218] [Optimizing Byzantine Node Placement in Decentralized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01495)
*Edoardo Gabrielli,Gabriele Tolomei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BPI（拜占庭放置影响力）指标，用于在去中心化联邦学习（DFL）中系统性地评估和优化拜占庭节点的放置位置，从而揭示拜占庭放置这一被忽视但关键的威胁维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DFL安全评估主要关注拜占庭参与者如何行为（攻击策略），却忽略了哪些参与者被攻陷（放置位置）。由于聚合过程分布在通信图上，拜占庭节点的放置位置直接决定了恶意影响的传播路径和范围，这一维度在现有威胁模型中未被充分建模。

Method: 将拜占庭放置建模为对抗者的显式决策，在固定预算下选择最大化对诚实节点有限时间内影响的参与者集合。提出了基于实际gossip传播动态的集合级度量BPI，量化训练期间诚实节点对拜占庭来源的累积暴露。相比基于节点中心性启发式的准则，BPI直接考虑加权多跳传播及被攻陷节点间的交互，并开发了高效优化算法。

Result: 在六类异构图族上，针对无目标模型投毒和后门攻击进行评估。BPI引导的放置策略在不同网络结构下一致地识别出高破坏性配置，并且在放宽线性gossip假设（采用拜占庭鲁棒聚合）时仍然有效。

Conclusion: 拜占庭放置是DFL威胁模型和鲁棒性评估中关键但被低估的维度，BPI为这一维度提供了实用的系统化评估工具。

Abstract: Security evaluations of decentralized federated learning (DFL) typically focus on how Byzantine participants behave, while largely overlooking which participants are compromised. Yet, because aggregation is distributed over a communication graph, the placement of Byzantine nodes determines how malicious influence propagates through the network. We therefore treat Byzantine placement as an explicit adversarial decision and formulate the attacker's objective as selecting, under a fixed compromise budget, the set of participants that maximizes its finite-time impact on honest nodes. To approximate this objective without executing the learning process for every candidate placement, we introduce Byzantine Placement Influence (BPI), a set-level measure derived from the actual gossip dynamics that quantifies the cumulative exposure of honest nodes to Byzantine sources over the training horizon. Unlike placement criteria based on node centrality heuristics, BPI directly accounts for weighted multi-hop propagation and interactions among compromised nodes. We develop efficient algorithms for optimizing BPI and evaluate them across six heterogeneous graph families, untargeted model poisoning, and backdoor attacks. BPI-guided placements consistently identify highly damaging configurations across different network structures and remain effective when the linear gossip assumption is relaxed through Byzantine-robust aggregation. Our results show that Byzantine placement is a critical but under-modeled dimension of DFL threat models and robustness evaluations.

</details>


### [219] [LatentPress: Context Compression Beyond Text and Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01507)
*Zhengze Zhou,Hejian Sang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LatentPress 提出了一种面向语言模型的第三种上下文表示——连续记忆token，直接将压缩后的对话历史和长文档以软token形式注入冻结解码器的输入嵌入层，无需推理时文本重建，实现4-16倍压缩，参数仅占解码器约0.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统压缩上下文通常以人类可读文本或渲染图像承载，即使消费者是语言模型也需要解码还原。作者旨在探索一种超越文本和视觉的、直接面向机器的上下文接口——连续软token表示。

Method: 采用一个与解码器匹配的小型编写器（writer），将对话历史和长文档压缩为连续记忆token，解码器通过输入嵌入接口直接读取；仅训练适配器（4.2M-26.2M参数，约占解码器0.1%）即可实现4-16倍压缩，无需推理时文本重建。

Result: 在LongMemEval上，7.70倍压缩下准确率0.504，优于未压缩证据的0.490，远超文本摘要（0.184）和OCR压缩（0.426至0.312）；在LongBench-QA上，域内编写器在4-8倍压缩时匹配或超越原始上下文阅读，16倍时略逊；写入每条对话仅需43ms，比文本摘要或OCR重建快约一个数量级，读取速度快5-9倍。

Conclusion: 通过零样本迁移实验（UltraChat到LongMemEval记忆QA、LongMemEval派生QA到LongBench文档域），验证了直接软token作为面向机器的上下文接口的可行性，确立了其在文本和视觉之外的实用价值。

Abstract: Compressed context is usually carried as human-readable text or as rendered images that must be decoded, even when its consumer is a language model. We introduce LatentPress, which writes conversational histories and long documents into a third representation: continuous memory tokens that a frozen decoder reads directly through its input-embedding interface, with no text reconstruction at inference. A small reader-matched writer compresses $4$-$16\times$ while training only an adapter (4.2M-26.2M parameters, $\sim\!0.1\%$ of the decoder). On LongMemEval, LatentPress reaches $0.504$ accuracy at $7.70\times$ compression versus $0.490$ for uncompressed evidence, outperforming text summaries (0.184) and OCR-based compression (0.426 to 0.312). On LongBench-QA, in-domain writers match or exceed raw-context reading at $4$-$8\times$ compression, while $16\times$ trails raw. Writing takes 43ms per conversation, roughly an order of magnitude faster than text summarization or OCR reconstruction, and reading is $5$-$9\times$ faster than raw context or cached OCR. We validate the interface under two transfer settings, zero-shot from UltraChat to LongMemEval memory QA and from LongMemEval-derived QA to unseen LongBench document domains, establishing direct soft tokens as a practical machine-facing context interface beyond text and vision. The implementation of the experiments could be found at: https://github.com/xuyd16ai/context_softtoken_compress .

</details>


### [220] [Quantum Sparse Autoencoders for Q-Matrix Estimation in Cognitive Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01537)
*Arif Hassan Zidan,Yi Pan,Bowen Guo,Xiang Li,Yu Bao,Yingfeng Wang,Tianming Liu,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个将量子机器学习（QML）应用于认知诊断的量子稀疏自编码器（QSAE），用于Q矩阵估计。QSAE将学生的二元响应向量编码到量子线路中，压缩为稀疏潜在表示来估计Q矩阵。在60个模拟数据集和9个真实数据集上与经典自编码器（CAE）对比，QSAE虽然在部分模拟条件下准确率略低，但在49/60个条件下方差更低（更稳定），且在9个真实数据集中的6个上优于CAE。核心发现是QML在该场景的主要价值并非普遍提升准确率，而是增强鲁棒性和探索真实数据潜在结构复杂性的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Q矩阵在认知诊断和教育数据挖掘中至关重要，它指定了每个评估项目所需的潜在技能。然而，当评估涉及多个相关技能且真实作答模式偏离理想生成假设时，数据驱动的Q矩阵估计仍面临重大挑战，需要探索新的估计方法。

Method: 提出量子稀疏自编码器（QSAE）：将每个学生的二元响应向量通过编码器嵌入量子线路，压缩为稀疏潜在表示，再将该表示映射到Q矩阵。这是量子机器学习在认知诊断中的首次应用。基准对比采用经典自编码器（CAE），分别在60个模拟数据集和9个真实评估数据集上进行实验。

Result: CAE在部分模拟条件下实现更高的平均准确率，但QSAE在60个模拟条件下的49个中展现出更低的方差（稳定性更强）。在9个真实评估数据集中，QSAE在6个数据集上优于CAE。

Conclusion: QML在此应用场景中的主要优势并非普遍性地提升准确率，而是增强模型鲁棒性以及提升对真实数据中潜在结构复杂性的探索能力。

Abstract: Q-matrices play a central role in cognitive diagnosis within educational data mining (EDM), specifying which latent skills each assessment item requires. Data-driven Q-matrix estimation remains challenging when assessments involve many correlated skills and when real response patterns depart from idealized generative assumptions. We introduce a novel quantum sparse autoencoder (QSAE) for Q-matrix estimation, which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first application of quantum machine learning (QML) to cognitive diagnosis. Overall, the QSAE embeds each student's binary response vector into a quantum circuit using an encoder, compresses it into a sparse latent representation, and maps that representation to the Q-matrix. We benchmark the QSAE against a classical autoencoder (CAE) across 60 simulated datasets and 9 real-world assessment datasets. The results reveal complementary strengths. Although the CAE partially achieves higher average accuracy under several simulation conditions, the QSAE is substantially more stable across replications, exhibiting lower variance in 49 of the 60 conditions. Moreover, on real assessment data, the QSAE outperforms the CAE on 6 of the 9 datasets. These findings suggest that the principal advancement of QML in this setting is not universal accuracy improvement, but enhanced robustness and capability to explore latent-structure complexity in real datasets.

</details>


### [221] [NashDreamer: Model-Based Reinforcement Learning for Zero-Sum Imperfect-Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01549)
*Tomáš Holeček,Viliam Lisý*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NashDreamer，一个用于两人零和不完全信息博弈的基于模型的强化学习（MBRL）框架，通过集中式多智能体循环状态空间模型（MARSSM）解耦环境动态与玩家策略对观测的影响，显著提升了样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MBRL在单智能体领域取得了显著成果，但扩展到竞争性不完全信息博弈（IIGs）方面仍未得到充分探索。在多智能体环境中，对手引起的非平稳性使学习过程复杂化，且分散式模型学习面临严重的可辨识性障碍，作者认为这使得集中式模型学习成为数学上的必然选择。

Method: 提出了NashDreamer框架，包括一个集中式多智能体循环状态空间模型（MARSSM），该模型将环境动力学与玩家策略对其个体观测的影响进行解耦。该框架设计为可使用任意策略梯度算法，并在理想化模型下继承其向纳什均衡收敛的保证。

Result: 在四个基准游戏上的实证评估表明，NashDreamer在训练早期相比无模型基线显著提高了样本效率。此外，论文从理论上分析了该架构的优化景观，识别出Dreamer系列算法在随机环境中容易遭受后验坍塌的脆弱性。

Conclusion: NashDreamer为两人零和IIGs提供了有效的MBRL框架，验证了集中式模型学习的必要性，并显著提升了样本效率。同时，论文指出了Dreamer系列算法在后验坍塌方面的理论局限，将该问题留作开放挑战。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) has achieved remarkable results in single-agent domains, yet its extension to competitive imperfect information games (IIGs) remains underexplored. In multi-agent settings, opponent-induced non-stationarity complicates the learning process, and decentralized model learning faces severe identifiability barriers, which we argue make centralized model learning a mathematical necessity. Building on this analysis, we propose NashDreamer, a principled MBRL framework for two-player zero-sum IIGs. It introduces a centralized Multi-Agent Recurrent State-Space Model (MARSSM) that decouples environment dynamics from the effect of players' strategies on their individual observations. NashDreamer is designed to use arbitrary policy gradient algorithms and inherits their convergence guarantees towards Nash equilibria under an idealized model. Empirical evaluations across four benchmark games demonstrate that NashDreamer substantially improves sample efficiency over model-free baselines early in the training. Finally, we theoretically analyze the architecture's optimization landscape, identifying the vulnerability of the Dreamer family of algorithms to posterior collapse in stochastic environments. We leave it as an open challenge.

</details>


### [222] [A Mathematical Theory of Reusable Neural Bases for Network Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01550)
*Binshuai Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出线性可复用神经基架构（LRNBA），通过将每个网络块表示为共享神经基的线性组合，在保持训练稳定的同时大幅提升参数效率并降低内存开销，支持在相同参数预算下构建更深更宽的网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型AI模型在训练和推理中面临严重的内存瓶颈，亟需一种能提升参数效率并减少内存成本的方法。

Method: 受RNN设计启发，将每个网络块表示为一组共享神经基的线性组合，实现高网络压缩率，同时保持稳定的训练动力学。

Result: 实验表明该架构在相同参数预算下可构建更深更宽的网络，收敛速度与经典架构相当甚至更快，并能获得更低的损失值，训练动力学保持稳定。

Conclusion: LRNBA是一种有效的参数高效架构，能够在不牺牲训练稳定性的前提下显著降低内存成本。

Abstract: As large AI models become increasingly prevalent across a wide range of applications, memory cost has become a critical bottleneck in both training and inference. To mitigate this issue, we introduce the Linear Reusable Neural Bases Architecture (LRNBA), a novel framework aimed at improving parameter efficiency and reducing memory cost. Inspired by recurrent neural network (RNN) designs, the core idea of our approach is to represent each network block as a linear combination of a shared set of neural bases, thereby enjoying highly network compression rate while maintaining stable training. The proposed architecture allows for the construction of significantly wider and deeper networks under the same parameter budget. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our model achieves comparable or even faster convergence and lower loss than classical architectures, while maintaining stable training dynamics.

</details>


### [223] [Retrieved but not ranked: surface-form bias in structural retrieval, from mathematics to agent trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01556)
*Nabira Rashid,Manolis Kellis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了嵌入模型在"表面形式与语义含义被刻意分离"条件下的检索能力，横跨竞赛数学（MathNet-Retrieve）和具身智能体轨迹（ALFWorld）两个无关领域。结果显示：在数学伪装检索上，产量级嵌入器在最强伪装层级的严格Hit@1为0.0%，但正确答案几乎总在前10名内；在轨迹检索中，当金标准涉及不同对象时模型命中率趋近超几何随机水平，对象与容器均需不同时则低于随机水平。词汇重排序作为对照在数学中有害、在轨迹中有益；LLM重排序可恢复数学5-63%、轨迹43-76%的差距，但部分恢复来自模型对知名竞赛的记忆。下游配对实验表明，检索质量对求解器准确率几乎无影响，因其69.5%的零样本准确率实质上是截断代理（对已完成答案正确率97-100%），没有提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入检索评估大多在表面形式与语义含义一致或接近的设定下进行，难以揭示模型是否真正理解底层结构而非依赖字面token匹配。本文旨在通过将表面形式与含义刻意分离的对抗性基准，检验嵌入模型在数学题和具身智能体轨迹两个领域中的结构理解能力，并评估不同重排序策略（词汇与LLM）的效果及其背后的机制（如记忆而非推理）。

Method: 设计两个统一协议下的检索基准：竞赛数学MathNet-Retrieve（500查询，117,088项语料）与ALFWorld衍生轨迹集（118查询，336条轨迹），均有逐步加重的表面伪装层级，使正确项与查询在形式上分离但结构上对应。使用三个生产级别嵌入器进行检索，比较严格Hit@1与词汇相似度；设置词汇重排序器和LLM重排序器作为对照或干预，统计配对差异置信区间；并设计下游配对实验（210查询，评分者一致性96-99%），对比检索从oracle到最差情况对求解器的影响。

Result: 数学上失败完全：最重伪装层的Hit@1为0.0%（CI [0.0,0.0]），但正确答案95-99.8%的近失案例中胜出项在词汇上比正确答案更接近查询。轨迹上，金标准需不同对象时命中率趋近甚至低于随机水平。词汇重排序在数学中有害、在轨迹中有益（缩小26-36%差距）；LLM重排序在两个领域均有效（数学恢复5-63%，轨迹43-76%），21个单元格方向全部为正，但效应量、层级分布和离群裁判随领域而变。数学上的恢复集中于知名竞赛（+19.8点，CI [+6.7,+33.2]），表明含记忆成分。下游实验：检索策略对求解器无显著影响（McNemar p=0.678），因为求解器的69.5%准确率本质上是截断代理。

Conclusion: 生产级嵌入模型在检索中锚定于字面token而非任务结构，面对对抗性表面变化时完全失败；词汇重排序的符号可作为判断基准表面变异是对抗性还是附带性的指标。LLM重排序能部分恢复差距，但在数学中依赖记忆。最关键的发现是，在数学检索这一任务设定中，从"完美检索到最差检索"均不改变下游求解表现，说明这类配对任务无法区分检索质量，需重新设计能真实反映检索价值的评估协议。

Abstract: We evaluate embedding retrieval where surface form and meaning are pulled apart on purpose: retrieving items that share underlying structure but not wording, in two unrelated domains under one protocol, competition mathematics (MathNet-Retrieve; 500 queries, 117,088-item corpus) and embodied-agent trajectories (ALFWorld-derived; 118 queries, 336 trajectories). In mathematics the failure is complete: strict Hit@1 at the heaviest disguise tier is 0.0% for both production embedders (bootstrap 95% CI [0.0, 0.0]) while the correct item sits in the top 10 nearly always, and in 95.2 to 99.8% of misses the winner is more lexically similar to the query than the correct answer. In trajectories, where surface variation is incidental, the same models land at or near hypergeometric chance when gold must involve a different object, and below chance for all three embedders once gold must differ in object and receptacle: retrieval anchors on literal tokens, not task structure. A lexical reranker control hurts in mathematics and helps in trajectories (closing 26 to 36% of the gap, CIs excluding zero); its sign reveals whether a benchmark's surface variation is adversarial or incidental. An LLM reranker recovers 5 to 63% of the gap in mathematics and 43 to 76% in trajectories; direction replicates across three judges (all 21 cells positive), but effect sizes, tier profiles, and the outlier judge change with domain (paired differences excluding zero everywhere). Mathematics gains concentrate on well-known competitions (+19.8 points, CI [+6.7, +33.2], one of six cells), so part of the recovery is memorization. In a paired downstream experiment (210 queries, graders at 96 to 99% agreement), oracle retrieval was indistinguishable from adversarially bad retrieval (McNemar p = 0.678); the solver's 69.5% zero-shot accuracy is largely a truncation proxy (97 to 100% on finished answers), leaving no headroom.

</details>


### [224] [Gradient-Update Mismatch: Rethinking Conflict-Free Training of Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01558)
*Jing Xiao,Xinhai Chen,Qinglin Wang,Menghan Jia,Zhiquan Lai,Dongsheng Li,Jie Liu,Tiejun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出梯度更新错位（GUM）问题，指优化器对梯度手术构建的无冲突方向进行变换后可能再次引入冲突。作者提出梯度更新对齐（GUA）方法，将优化器更新投影到无冲突锥上，并调整优化器内部状态，在PINN训练中实现无冲突更新并显著提升多种梯度手术方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN训练中物理残差与初边值条件的损失项常产生冲突梯度，梯度手术方法虽能构建无冲突方向，但优化器（如动量、自适应缩放、预处理、解耦权重衰减）的变换会破坏这一属性，导致优化器实际更新仍存在梯度冲突。

Method: GUA方法将优化器的更新投影到由损失特定梯度诱导的无冲突锥上，得到对齐更新并应用于参数；对含内部状态的优化器，GUA将状态调整为从应用更新反向重建的目标，确保后续更新的一致性。

Result: 实验发现GUM在动量、自适应和曲率类优化器中普遍存在，冲突率最高达86.3%。在所有PINN设置中，GUA实现了无冲突的应用更新，一致提升了多种梯度手术方法，个别设置下相对L2误差最多降低98.2%。

Conclusion: 梯度手术构建的无冲突方向在优化器变换后并不保序，需在优化器层面进行对齐校正；GUA通过在更新层面施加投影并同步调整优化器状态，有效解决了PINN训练中的梯度冲突问题。

Abstract: Training Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) requires jointly optimizing physics residual and initial/boundary condition loss terms, which often induce conflicting gradients. Gradient surgery methods mitigate this issue by constructing directions from loss-specific gradients to reduce conflict before optimizer transformation. However, even when the constructed direction is conflict-free, this property may not be preserved after optimizer transformation. Let $a_t$ denote the direction constructed by gradient surgery, $u_t$ the optimizer proposal, and $\mathcal{C}_t$ the conflict-free cone induced by the loss-specific gradients. We show that modern optimizers can transform $a_t$ through mechanisms such as historical state, adaptive scaling, preconditioning, or decoupled weight decay, so $a_t \in \mathcal{C}_t$ does not generally imply $u_t \in \mathcal{C}_t$. We refer to this optimizer-induced discrepancy in conflict-freeness between $a_t$ and $u_t$ as Gradient-Update Mismatch (GUM). Accordingly, we propose Gradient-Update Alignment (GUA), which projects $u_t$ onto $\mathcal{C}_t$ to obtain the aligned update $p_t$ and applies $p_t$ to the parameters. When the optimizer maintains internal state, GUA further adjusts this state toward targets reconstructed from the applied update. We conduct extensive experiments and find that GUM is widespread across momentum, adaptive, and curvature-based optimizers, with conflict rates reaching up to 86.3%. Across all PINN settings, GUA achieves conflict-free applied updates and consistently improves various gradient surgery methods, reducing the relative $L_2$ error by up to 98.2% in individual settings. Data and code are available at https://github.com/JingXiao10/GUA.

</details>


### [225] [The Structure of Quantization Damage in LLMs: Why the Next Bit Should Be Spent Globally](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01587)
*Jundong Hu,Shekar Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLM）的后训练量化（PTQ）中精度损失的分布问题。通过因果混合精度干预实验（逐一将每层提升至8-bit并测量精度恢复）在9个开源模型、4个架构家族上进行验证，发现三种直觉假设（量化损伤存在于任务电路、模型计算位置或权重统计中）均无法预测哪些层能从精度恢复中获益。实际恢复是弥散性的——8/9的模型用约一半的层即可恢复75%的精度差距，仅Qwen3-8B呈现出高度集中性。在匹配的精度预算下，全局细粒度量化在所有8个group-128兼容模型上（21-52个点的差距）优于局部修复最可恢复的层。两个次要发现：残差受预算限制（8-bit在RTN、GPTQ和AWQ下近乎无损），峰值恢复位置与架构族内相关但跨族不相关。结论是：在该预算设定下，全局细粒度优于选择性保护关键层；能够与量化损伤相关的廉价信号并不一定指明精度恢复有效的位置，必须通过因果干预来验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PTQ被广泛用于降低LLM推理成本，但其精度损失在不同模型上是不均匀的，且通常需要逐模型调优。作者希望理解量化损伤实际发生在何处，以及如何有效分配少量额外的精度预算，从而为PTQ策略提供更科学的基础而非依赖经验性调参。

Method: 以因果混合精度干预作为ground truth方法——依次将每一层提升至8-bit并测量恢复的精度。在9个开源模型（涵盖4个架构家族）上检验三种直觉假设：（1）量化损伤位于任务电路；（2）位于模型计算位置；（3）位于权重统计特征。同时对比两种精度预算分配策略：全局细粒度量化和局部修复最可恢复的层。

Result: 三种假设均无法预测精度恢复受益层的位置。恢复呈弥散性分布：8/9的模型中约一半层即可恢复75%的精度差距，唯Qwen3-8B高度集中。在匹配预算下，全局细粒度量化在所有group-128兼容模型上优于局部修复策略21-52个点（包括集中型的Qwen3-8B）。此外，8-bit量化为近无损，峰值恢复位置在架构家族内相关但跨家族不相关。

Conclusion: 在该预算设定下，全局细粒度是比选择性保护关键层更好的默认策略。更广义的启示是：与量化损伤相关的廉价代理信号不一定能识别精度恢复有效的位置，必须通过因果干预来测试。这挑战了"定位关键层并优先保护"这一直觉性但可能错误的工程思路。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is widely used to reduce the cost of serving large language models (LLMs), but its accuracy cost is uneven and is often tuned per model. We study where quantization damage occurs and how to allocate a small additional precision budget. Using causal mixed-precision intervention as ground truth (raise each layer to 8-bit in turn and measure the accuracy it recovers) across 9 open-weight models in 4 architecture families, we test 3 intuitive hypotheses: that quantization damage lives in task circuits, where the model computes, or in weight statistics. None of them predicts which layers benefit from restored precision. Recovery is instead diffuse: for 8 of 9 models, recovering 75% of the gap takes roughly half the layers; the lone exception, Qwen3-8B, is sharply concentrated. At a matched precision budget, spending it globally on finer quantization granularity beats locally repairing the most recoverable layers for all 8 group-128-compatible models (all but OpenLLaMA, whose width rules out group-128), by 21-52 points, including the concentrated Qwen3-8B. We report 2 secondary findings: the residual is budget-limited (8-bit is near-lossless in our evaluation across RTN, GPTQ, and AWQ), and the location of peak recovery correlates with architecture within a family, though not across families. Within this budget setting, global granularity is a better default than selectively protecting critical layers. More broadly, cheap signals that correlate with quantization damage do not necessarily identify where restoring precision improves accuracy; this must be tested with causal intervention.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [226] [Can LLMs Design Video Coding Tools? A Case Study on Planar Mode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01535)
*Yingwen Zhang,Meng Wang,Liqiang He,Shiqi Wang*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文通过实证案例研究探讨了大型语言模型（LLM）能否设计视频编码工具，具体聚焦于视频编码标准中长期存在的Planar帧内预测模式。在生成-评估循环框架下，LLM生成的Planar预测器在VVenC快速预设下实现了0.18%的码率节省（仅0.4%复杂度开销），并在ECM中通过两种集成策略在低分辨率设定下均获得编码增益，提供了LLM驱动编码工具设计的初步证据与实用见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频编码工具的设计极具挑战性，因为工具修改涉及复杂的算法耦合关系。作者希望探索LLM是否能够胜任这一高度专业化的任务，而非仅局限于代码生成或自然语言处理等常见领域，以拓展LLM在系统级算法设计中的应用边界。

Method: 采用生成-评估循环架构：LLM生成新的Planar预测器实现代码，编码器（VVenC）通过实际编码实验评估其编码性能，再将评估反馈回传给LLM以迭代优化生成结果。在VVenC中直接替换默认Planar模式；在ECM中利用新引入的方向性Planar模式，测试了"直接替换"和"作为新预测模式引入（含新语法元素）"两种集成策略。

Result: 在VVenC快速预设下，LLM生成的Planar模式优于传统方案，实现0.18%的码率节省且仅增加0.4%的复杂度；在ECM中，两种集成策略在受约束的低分辨率设定下均能带来编码增益。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了LLM能够参与视频编码工具设计的初步证据，展示了其在算法优化方面的潜力，同时也揭示了面临的开放性挑战，为未来LLM辅助编码标准化工作奠定了基础。

Abstract: This paper explores whether large language models (LLMs) can design video coding tools, a highly challenging task due to the intricate algorithmic coupling of tool modifications. In particular, we present an empirical case study on the Planar mode, a long-standing intra prediction tool in video coding standards. Our experiments operate within a generation-and-evaluation loop, with the LLM generating new Planar predictors, encoder trials evaluating their coding performance, and the LLM re-generating refined implementations based on the evaluation feedback. We first examine directly replacing the default Planar mode in the Fraunhofer Versatile Video Encoder (VVenC) under its faster preset. Experimental results demonstrate that the LLM-generated mode can outperform the conventional Planar mode on this lightweight toolset, achieving 0.18% bitrate savings with 0.4% complexity overhead on the standard benchmark. We further extend our evaluation to the Enhanced Compression Model (ECM). Leveraging newly introduced directional Planar modes, we investigate two integration strategies: directly replacing them, and introducing the LLM-generated predictor as an additional prediction mode with new syntax elements. The empirical results suggest that both strategies can yield coding gains under a constrained low-resolution setting. Overall, this study offers preliminary evidence and practical insights, highlighting both the potential and open challenges of LLM-based coding tool design.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [227] [Jailbreaking Text-to-Image Models Through Cracks: Navigating Heterogeneous Safety Filters via Multi-Agent Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01168)
*Kaiyan Wen,Shijie Zhang,Lu Yu,Guangdong Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Detection Surface统一几何框架，刻画多层T2I安全过滤器的决策边界及其交叉冲突，并基于此提出CRACK多智能体辩论框架，通过攻击、防御、裁判三智能体协同迭代，实现自适应越狱搜索，在复合防御下达到99.63%的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I越狱研究要么针对单一过滤器优化，要么用聚合反馈查询完整管线，难以识别活跃约束并适应跨安全层的冲突。多层异构安全堆栈（文本过滤器、图像分类器、跨模态检测器）之间的不一致性使越狱搜索面临非凸、稀疏且层间冲突的难题，亟需一个能刻画这些层间相互作用并指导自适应搜索的统一框架。

Method: 提出Detection Surface统一几何框架来表征异构T2I安全过滤器的决策边界及其联合效应；基于该分析提出CRACK多智能体辩论框架，将越狱搜索分解为探索、诊断和仲裁三个阶段，协调攻击智能体、防御智能体和裁判智能体进行迭代式提示词变异、逐层诊断反馈获取，以及通过奖励引导的优化调整变异策略。

Result: 在多个T2I模型、数据集和安全配置上，CRACK在复合防御下达到最高99.63%的攻击成功率（ASR），相比现有方法需要更少的查询次数，同时保持语义保真度。

Conclusion: Detection Surface框架有效揭示了多层安全过滤器的联合决策几何结构，CRACK通过多智能体辩论能够自适应地应对跨层约束的动态变化，在更高效的同时实现了更高的攻击成功率，证明了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models remain vulnerable to jailbreak attacks that elicit Not-Safe-For-Work (NSFW) content, despite increasingly being guarded by heterogeneous, multi-layer safety stacks combining text filters, image classifiers, and cross-modal detectors. Existing jailbreak studies either optimize against individual filters or query the complete pipeline with aggregate feedback, making it difficult to identify the active constraint and adapt to conflicts across safety layers.In this paper, we introduce the \emph{Detection Surface}, a unified geometric framework that characterizes the decision boundaries induced by heterogeneous T2I safety filters and their joint effect on the jailbreak search space. This formulation reveals that successful evasion is governed by a sparse and non-convex region shaped by cross-layer conflicts, where mutations that bypass one filter may increase exposure to another. Motivated by this analysis, we propose \emph{CRACK}, a multi-agent debate framework for adaptive jailbreak search that decomposes jailbreak search into exploration, diagnosis, and arbitration. CRACK coordinates an Attack Agent, a Defense Agent, and a Judge Agent to iteratively generate prompt mutations, obtain layer-specific diagnostic feedback, and optimize mutation strategies through reward-guided refinement. Through repeated rounds of debate, CRACK adapts its search direction to the evolving cross-layer constraints while preserving the original harmful intent. Extensive experiments across multiple T2I models, datasets, and safety configurations show that CRACK achieves Attack Success Rates (ASR) of up to 99.63\% under composite defenses, while requiring fewer queries than existing methods and maintaining semantic fidelity.

</details>


### [228] [HyperWorld: Hypergraph-Structured State Serialization Improves Learned Textual World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00002)
*Yun-Jian Zhang,Chen-Wei Liang,Tian-Yi Zhang,Jian Ding,Yi-Lun Wu,Ao-Bo Li,Wei-Cong Su,Saifullah,Hong-Yu An,Mu-Jiang-Shan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HyperWorld，一项针对文本环境中状态序列化方式对世界模型影响的研究。作者比较了原始观测与三种符号化序列化方式（独立句、成对三元组、以实体为中心的超边单元），发现超边序列化在中小规模模型（0.5B–1.5B）及分布外场景下表现最佳，且在贪心规划中获得最高成功率，表明高阶状态组织是一种简单而有效的归纳偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在文本环境中，语言模型智能体需要通过序列化的状态描述来学习符号动作效果并预测环境动态。然而，状态序列化的结构（即信息如何组织和呈现给模型）对世界模型学习效果的影响尚未被充分探索。作者希望系统性地研究这一此前被忽视的关键因素。

Method: 作者设计了受控实验框架HyperWorld，使用相同的底层真实状态，但采用三种不同的符号化序列化方式呈现：独立句子、成对三元组、以及将多个相关事实围绕实体和关系分组的超边单元。所有变体使用相同的训练目标——给定状态和动作，预测符号效果或判断动作不可行。实验覆盖不同模型规模（0.5B–1.5B）、不同数据预算，以及分布内和分布外两种测试环境。

Result: 超边序列化在中小规模模型（0.5B–1.5B）和分布转移条件下带来最明显的增益。更大模型会缩小不同表示之间的差距；在分布内精确匹配任务上，成对三元组可匹敌甚至略微超越超边表示，但超边在分布外的事实F1分数最强，且在中小规模下实现了可行性检测与效果预测之间的最佳权衡。在贪心规划的下游任务中，超边世界模型成功率最高。

Conclusion: 高阶状态组织（超边单元）是一种简单但有效的归纳偏置，尤其当模型容量有限或测试环境与训练环境存在差异时，能显著提升学习型符号世界模型的性能。这为文本环境中的世界模型设计提供了实用的序列化策略指导。

Abstract: World models enable language-model agents to predict environment dynamics and plan before acting. In text environments, the model must learn symbolic action effects from serialized state descriptions, but the role of serialization structure remains underexplored. We present HyperWorld, a controlled study of state serialization for learned textual world models. We compare raw observations with three symbolic serializations of the same ground-truth state: independent sentences, pairwise triples, and entity-centered hyperedge units that group multiple related facts around entities and relations. All variants use the same training objective: given a state and an action, predict symbolic effects or judge the action infeasible. Across model scales, data budgets, and in-distribution and out-of-distribution test worlds, hyperedge serialization gives the clearest gains for 0.5B--1.5B models and under distribution shift. Larger models reduce the gap, and pairwise triples can match or slightly exceed hyperedges on in-distribution exact match, but hyperedges achieve the strongest out-of-distribution fact F1 and the best small-to-medium scale trade-off between feasibility detection and effect prediction. In downstream greedy planning, the hyperedge world model also attains the highest success rate among the tested representations. These results show that higher-order state organization is a simple but effective inductive bias for learned symbolic world models, especially when model capacity is limited or test environments differ from training.

</details>


### [229] [I-CARE: Analysis of interference-related phenomena in a controllable, diverse and representative unlearning setting for text-to-image models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00003)
*Leonardo Santiago Benitez Pereira,Marcos Escudero Viñolo,Luis Herranz Arribas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出I-CARE方法论，将"干扰"（interference）形式化为一等研究对象纳入生成式机器遗忘研究。I-CARE提供形式化的任务定义、评估指标和结果报告模板，而非提出新的基准或遗忘算法。该方法论旨在模型和遗忘算法演进过程中保持有效，并通过与最先进算法和常用数据集的可行性演示验证其实用性，同时提供开源软件实现和可视化Web界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式机器遗忘虽已快速发展，但遗忘过程中对语义相关概念（应保留的知识）产生的意外退化现象（即干扰）缺乏充分的刻画和一致的评估标准，阻碍了该领域的系统性研究与可复现性。

Method: I-CARE不依赖特定基准或算法，而是提供形式化的任务定义、度量指标和结果报告模板，将长期科学洞见与短期经验结果解耦，确保方法论的时效性。通过最先进的遗忘算法和常用数据集进行可行性演示以验证框架的实际应用。

Result: I-CARE能够跨多个遗忘设置进行有意义的干扰模式分析，实证证明了该框架的实际可应用性。同时提供了开源软件实现和基于Web的可视化图形界面，方便研究者无需编程或专业数据分析工具即可探索研究结果。

Conclusion: I-CARE为生成式机器遗忘中的干扰研究建立了系统化、可复现的方法论框架，区别于一次性基准或算法，为领域提供了可持续使用的标准化研究基础设施，推动了干扰现象作为独立研究问题的规范化探讨。

Abstract: Machine unlearning studies the removal of knowledge from an AI model, making the system forget a concept it previously learned. Despite rapid progress in generative machine unlearning, the unintended degradation of semantically related concepts that should have been retained (henceforth, interference) remains poorly characterized and inconsistently evaluated. This paper introduces I-CARE, a methodology that formalizes interference as a first-class object of study in generative unlearning. Rather than proposing a new benchmark or unlearning algorithm, I-CARE provides formal definitions for tasks, metrics, and templates for reporting results, enabling the systematic and reproducible study of interference across unlearning settings. While our methodology is designed to remain valid as models and unlearning algorithms evolve, decoupling long-term scientific insight from transient empirical results, we present a feasibility demonstration with state-of-the-art algorithms and frequently used datasets. The results demonstrate that I-CARE enables meaningful analysis of interference patterns across multiple unlearning settings, establishing the practical applicability of the framework. The software implementation of the methodology is provided in an open-source framework, together with a web-based graphical interface that enables exploration of the outcomes of this study without requiring direct interaction with the codebase or specialized data analysis tools.

</details>


### [230] [Discrete-Time MDP Modeling for Multi-Item Capacitated Lot Sizing with Stochastic Demand Timing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00004)
*Léa Bayati,Mohamed Dahmoune,Melek Rodoplu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究有限时域内多项目有容量限制的批量生产计划问题，其中需求量是确定性的，但需求到达的时间是随机的。每个需求在其已知时间窗口内发生一次，且必须在截止日期前满足。该问题被建模为离散时间马尔可夫决策过程（DTMDP），并提出了遗传算法（GA）来搜索可行的状态反馈策略。在330个基准实例上，GA与精确随机解的平均最优性差距约3.44%，在90个困难实例上保持低于5%的最优性差距阈值，平均优化加速比为6.89±1.41（95%置信水平）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献通常假设需求到达时间已知或采用某些近似简化处理，但实际生产环境中需求到达时间往往具有不确定性。本研究旨在深入理解随机时序对计算复杂度的影响，并提出一种高效的求解方法，使问题在实践规模上可解。

Method: 1) 将问题建模为有限时域多项目有容量限制的批量生产计划问题（需求数量确定但到达周期随机）；2) 将随机问题建模为离散时间马尔可夫决策过程（DTMDP），详细定义了状态空间、可行动作、转移核和单期成本函数；3) 将每个随机实例与确定性对应实例（以最可能的到达周期替换到达分布）进行对比，以分离随机时序的计算效应；4) 提出遗传算法（GA）搜索可行状态反馈策略，并在DTMDP转移模型下精确评估每个策略；5) 采用经验Bellman时间回归来估计无法精确求解的实例的精确求解时间，并外推GA的预期加速比。

Result: 实验表明：随机时序会显著增加状态数量、转移数量、求解时间和内存压力；在330个基准实例上，GA与精确解的（可用时）平均最优性差距约为3.44%；在90个困难实例上，GA保持在5%最优性差距阈值以下，平均优化加速比为6.89±1.41（95%置信水平）。

Conclusion: 遗传算法作为一种求解具有随机需求到达时序的批量生产计划问题的有效方法，在实际规模上可在可接受的次优性范围内（约3.44%）实现显著的计算加速（约6.89倍）。研究同时揭示了随机时序带来的计算挑战，为后续研究提供了量化参考。

Abstract: This paper studies a finite-horizon multi-item capacitated lot-sizing problem in which demand quantities are deterministic, while demand-arrival periods are stochastic. Each demand occurs once within a known time window and must be satisfied no later than its deadline. The proposed model makes production and allocation decisions at the demand level, allowing it to represent capacity competition, demand-specific backlog, and allocation-dependent inventory dynamics. The stochastic problem is formulated as a discrete-time Markov decision process (DTMDP), including the state space, feasible actions, transition kernel, and one-period cost function. To isolate the computational effect of stochastic timing, each stochastic instance is first compared with a deterministic counterpart in which each arrival distribution is replaced by its most likely arrival period. This comparison shows that stochastic timing substantially increases the number of states, the number of transitions, solution time, and memory pressure. A genetic algorithm (GA) is then proposed for the stochastic-timing problem. The GA searches over feasible state-feedback policies and evaluates each policy exactly under the DTMDP transition model. Computational experiments on 330 benchmark instances show that the GA remains close to the exact stochastic solution whenever the latter is available, with an average optimality gap of about $3.44\%$. On the difficult benchmark instances, comprising 90 test cases, the GA remains below the $5\%$ optimality-gap threshold and achieves an average optimization speedup of $6.89 \pm 1.41$ at the $95\%$ confidence level. For instances that cannot be solved exactly on the available hardware, an empirical Bellman-time regression is used to estimate the missing exact resolution time and extrapolate the expected GA speedup.

</details>


### [231] [Incremental Risk Assessment of Progressive Elder Financial Scams via Instruction-Tuned Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00005)
*Parviz Ghafariasl,Weimin Fu,Xiaolong Guo,Shing I. Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于累积轮次的风险评估框架，通过对多轮对话进行增量式聚合并在每个阶段重新评估风险，实现对老年人金融诈骗的动态监控。构建了涵盖投资、慈善和技术支持三类诈骗场景的多轮对话数据集，并在四种小型语言模型（Phi-4、LLaMA-3.2、DeepSeek-R1、Qwen3）上进行了微调和评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对老年人的金融诈骗日益通过文本和语音渠道（如邮件、短信、电话）发生，诈骗过程跨越多个对话轮次逐渐展开。由于风险信号在各轮次中逐步出现，需要能够在资源受限环境下持续更新风险评估的模型，以支持有效的诈骗检测。

Method: 提出了累积轮次风险评估框架，逐一聚合对话轮次并在每一步重新评估风险；构建了多轮对话数据集，涵盖投资、慈善和技术支持诈骗场景，每段对话含2-8轮并在每个累积阶段标注定性风险等级、连续风险得分、解释性理由和安全建议；在统一训练框架下微调四种小型语言模型评估其性能。

Result: 微调后的小型模型能够捕捉与欺诈相关的语言线索及跨轮升级模式，同时保持紧凑的架构以适配移动端和资源受限环境。其中Phi-4和LLaMA-3.2在其参数规模下取得了更强的轮次感知风险评估性能。

Conclusion: 结构化累积建模能够在面向部署的场景中支持增量式诈骗风险评估，同时突出展示了紧凑型语言模型在隐私保护和设备端欺诈防护方面的潜力。

Abstract: Financial scams targeting older adults increasingly occur through text and voice channels such as email, SMS, and phone calls, unfolding over multiple conversational turns that begin with impersonation or casual contact, escalate through trust building and urgency, and culminate in requests for sensitive information or financial transfers. Because risk signals emerge incrementally across turns, effective detection requires models that continuously update risk estimates under resource-constrained deployment settings. We propose a cumulative turn-based risk assessment framework that incrementally aggregates conversational turns and re-estimates risk at each step, enabling dynamic scam monitoring across progressively evolving conversations. A multi-turn dialogue dataset is constructed to cover investment, charity, and tech support scam scenarios, with each dialogue containing two to eight turns and annotated at every cumulative stage with a qualitative risk level, a continuous risk score, an explanatory rationale, and a safety recommendation. Four small language models (Phi-4, LLaMA-3.2, DeepSeek-R1, and Qwen3) are fine-tuned and evaluated under a unified training framework. Fine-tuned small models capture fraud-related linguistic cues and cross-turn escalation patterns while maintaining compact architectures suitable for mobile and resource-constrained deployment settings. Among the evaluated models, Phi-4 and LLaMA-3.2 achieve stronger turn-aware risk estimation performance relative to their parameter scale. These results suggest that structured cumulative modeling can support incremental scam risk assessment in deployment-oriented settings while highlighting the potential of compact language models for privacy-aware and on-device fraud protection.

</details>


### [232] [Long-Horizon State Tracking in LLMs: Executing MD5 through a Deep Sequence of Dependent Tool Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00012)
*Dheeraj Mohandas Pai,Lu Xian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过让LLM逐步计算MD5哈希（196次依赖工具调用、64轮、4个32位字），干净地验证了模型在长时程任务中携带精确中间状态的能力。实验表明gpt-oss-120b能在多数完成运行中正确携带状态并返回正确摘要；在最强设置下用LLM替代每个原始工具（驱动者+工作者计算完整哈希），发现保留模型自身推理上下文和对思考型工作者进行投票是成功的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有agent基准虽然报告端到端成功率，但混淆了状态跟踪困难与指令理解，缺少隔离状态跟踪的对照组，且易受幻觉最终答案等捷径影响，无法解释长任务失败的原因。长时程任务中每步依赖上一步时错误会级联放大，LLM能否在多次工具调用中携带精确中间状态本身尚未被明确验证。

Method: 让模型逐步计算MD5哈希：196个依赖工具调用分64轮进行，在上下文中携带4个32位字(a,b,c,d)。MD5从零实现（RFC 1321），每个调用与ground-truth轨迹对齐，逐位检查摘要，隔离纯簿记错误。测试gpt-oss-120b（混合专家，~5.5B活跃参数）；最强设置下用第二个LLM替换每个原始工具（驱动者+工作者无精确算术oracle计算完整哈希），关键变量为保留上下文推理及对思考型工作者进行投票。

Result: gpt-oss-120b在温度0、短固定提示下，能在多数完成的运行中跨越全部196次调用携带完整状态并返回正确摘要。在最强设置（无算术oracle）中，成功取决于两个不改变权重的因素：每轮保留模型自身推理于上下文，以及对启用思考的工作者进行投票以消除模运算失误。残余失败按来源分类定位为状态携带、算术与提供服务三类。

Conclusion: 该研究表明通过精心设计的实验（MD5逐步计算）可以干净地隔离和评估LLM在长时程多工具调用中的精确状态跟踪能力，并证实即使仅约5.5B活跃参数也能完成196步级联依赖的簿记任务。成功的关键因素是推理上下文保留与投票机制，而非权重改动；失败可按状态携带、算术和服务来源系统分类。

Abstract: Long-horizon tasks remain uncommon in large language model (LLM) evaluation, and for a reason: when each step depends on the last, per-step accuracy that looks excellent in isolation decays catastrophically, as errors cascade and the end-to-end failure probability grows sharply with length. Existing agentic benchmarks report end-to-end success but confound this state-tracking difficulty with instruction interpretation, give no control group that isolates it, and are vulnerable to shortcuts such as a hallucinated final answer, so they cannot say why a long run fails. Whether an LLM can carry exact intermediate state across many tool calls at all is itself not well established. We test this cleanly by having the model compute a cryptographic hash, MD5, step by step: a sequence of $196$ dependent tool calls over $64$ rounds while it carries four $32$-bit words $(a,b,c,d)$ in its own context from one call to the next. Interpretation is trivial and, because we implement MD5 from scratch (RFC~1321), we align every call to the ground-truth trace and check the digest to the bit, so any failure is pure bookkeeping. gpt-oss-120b, a mixture-of-experts model with only $\sim$5.5B active parameters per token, at temperature $0$ with a short fixed prompt, carries the full state across all $196$ calls and returns the correct digest on a majority of completed runs. In the strongest setting we replace every primitive tool with a second LLM, so a driver and a worker compute the whole hash from scratch with no exact-arithmetic oracle in the loop. Two ingredients decide success and neither changes the weights: keeping the model's own reasoning in its context each turn, and voting over a thinking-enabled worker to remove its modular-arithmetic slips. We localize the residual failures by origin, separating state-carrying from arithmetic and from serving.

</details>


### [233] [OpenAgentFlow: Enabling System-Wide Safety Boundaries for Heterogeneous AI Agent Fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00015)
*Dongsheng Chen,Xiangyu Zhao,Xin Yao,Xuetao Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenAgentFlow，一种针对异构AI智能体系统的控制平面/行动平面架构，在操作提交边界强制执行安全策略。通过将GUI操作、API调用、工具调用和LLM生成调用标准化为统一的AgentEvent流，并通过共享的策略执行点进行路由，实现系统级的操作治理、审计和动态策略更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型驱动的AI智能体正从独立助手演变为异构系统，多个智能体共享同一用户或企业环境。现有安全防护仅覆盖单点（如提示词、工具调用、GUI操作），缺乏系统级的操作治理能力，难以应对跨多步骤操作流中涌现的风险，且审计支持和策略演进能力有限。

Method: 提出控制平面/行动平面分离的架构。行动平面将待执行的GUI操作、API调用、工具调用和LLM调用统一规范化为AgentEvent流；控制平面维护事件来源、会话状态、审计记录和可更新策略，每个事件通过共享的预执行策略执行点路由，从而实现统一的可治理操作流，新规则部署无需修改智能体、提示词或执行路径。

Result: 在Android平台上实现验证：300例操作事件基准测试中达到94.0%准确率和95.3%攻击拦截率；30例动态策略测试中27例符合期望行为；100例Android模拟器套件的98个追踪案例中实现90.8%原始准确率和92.9%追踪调整通过率（涵盖GUI、API和LLM规划场景）。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明OpenAgentFlow为异构AI智能体集群提供了实用的共享强制边界，能够有效统一治理多种类型的智能体生成操作，并支持灵活的策略动态更新和审计追踪。

Abstract: AI agents powered by large language models are evolving from isolated assistants into heterogeneous systems in which multiple agents, planners, controllers, and execution backends operate over the same user or enterprise environment. In such settings, safety becomes a system-level action-governance problem: deciding whether concrete agent-generated actions should be committed before they modify shared state. Existing safeguards cover prompts, tool calls, GUI actions, and agent-local behavior, but often leave enforcement fragmented, obscure risks that emerge across multi-step action flows, and provide limited support for auditability and policy evolution.
  We present OpenAgentFlow, a control-plane/action-plane architecture that enforces safety at the action-commit boundary. It normalizes pending GUI actions, API calls, tool calls, and LLM-generated invocations into a unified AgentEvent stream, routes each event through a shared pre-execution Policy Enforcement Point, and maintains provenance, session state, audit records, and updatable policies in the control plane. This creates a shared governable action stream and allows new rules to take effect without modifying agents, prompts, models, or execution paths.
  We instantiate OpenAgentFlow on Android. On a 300-case action-event benchmark, it achieves 94.0% accuracy and a 95.3% attack block rate. On a 30-case dynamic-policy suite, it matches expected behavior in 27 cases after new rules are installed. Across 98 traced cases from a 100-case Android emulator suite, it achieves 90.8% raw accuracy and a 92.9% trace-adjusted pass rate across GUI, API, and LLM-planned cases. These results show that OpenAgentFlow provides a practical shared enforcement boundary for heterogeneous AI agent fleets.

</details>


### [234] [SCAFFOLD: A Large-Scale Structured Dataset of Computer Science Research Figures with Diagram QA and Chain-of-Thought Reasoning Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00018)
*Ranjit Raut,Aarav Subedi,Sagun Rai,Sudan Jha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SCAFFOLD，一个大规模的结构化计算机科学研究图表数据集，包含图像、标题、上下文、问答对和思维链推理痕迹，用于训练视觉语言模型理解学术论文中的图表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机科学论文严重依赖图表（架构图、系统流程图、流水线示意图），这些图表往往比周围文本承载更多信息。但目前缺乏将这类特定图形与标题、上下文、问题、答案和逐步推理配对的公开数据集，而这正是训练视觉语言模型理解图表所必需的。

Method: 从arXiv计算机科学论文中，利用版面检测和PDF解析准备（图像、标题、上下文、问答、思维链）元组数据，并通过AI辅助问题生成步骤构建数据集。构建了三个规模的数据集：SCAFFOLD-157K（3,058篇论文、29,887个图表、157,387对）、SCAFFOLD-37K（36,797对）和SCAFFOLD-12K（12,000对）。

Result: 构建了覆盖3,058篇论文、29,887个图表的SCAFFOLD-157K大规模数据集，以及中等规模的SCAFFOLD-37K和小规模的SCAFFOLD-12K数据集，并使用SCAFFOLD-12K对Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct进行了基线实验。

Conclusion: SCAFFOLD提供了首个公开可用的、配对CS论文图表与详细推理注释的大规模结构化数据集，填补了该领域数据空白，为训练和理解学术图表视觉语言模型奠定了资源基础。

Abstract: Computer science papers rely heavily on diagrams: architecture drawings, system flowcharts, and pipeline schematics that often carry more information than the text around them. There is currently no public dataset that pairs this specific kind of figure with captions, context, questions, answers, and step-by-step reasoning, which is exactly what is needed to train a vision-language model to understand them. We present \textbf{SCAFFOLD}\footnote{https://github.com/theranjitraut/scaffold}, a large-scale structured dataset of computer science research figures with diagram QA and Chain-of-Thought reasoning traces. This dataset consists of (image, caption, context, question-answer, chain-of-thought) tuples from arXiv computer science papers prepared using layout detection and PDF parsing, with an AI-assisted question-generation step. The resulting large-sized SCAFFOLD-157K dataset spans 3,058 papers with 29,887 figures (157,387 pairs), a medium-sized SCAFFOLD-37K dataset (36,797 pairs), and a small-sized SCAFFOLD-12K dataset (12,000 pairs). We used SCAFFOLD-12K for baseline experiments on Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct.

</details>
